{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FMZcW26QXY8Epp8v8v54ms","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// Vendored verbatim from re-cinq/lore libs/shared/src (commit 4536b6c6f). Lore stays the source of truth; keep in sync.\n2\t// Pure parser for inline link parentheticals at the end of a spec.md statement (spec-test-coverage v3 §Decisions): trailing `([label](path#Lline), ...)`, split into test links (VALIDATED_BY) vs code links (IMPLEMENTED_BY).\n3\timport { posix } from \"node:path\";\n4\timport { isTestFile, isDocFile } from \"./test-paths.js\";\n5\timport { segmentStatements } from \"./spec-segment.js\";\n6\t/** Strip a leading `./` or `/` so repo-root-relative and dot-relative forms match. */\n7\texport function normalizePath(path) {\n8\t    return path.replace(/^\\.?\\/+/, \"\");\n9\t}\n10\t// A `../`-climbing href is relative to the spec's own directory (as GitHub renders it), resolved against dirname(specPath); both the graph binder and the require-spec-link ESLint index resolve through here so they agree.\n11\texport function resolveLinkPath(linkPath, specPath) {\n12\t    const stripped = linkPath.startsWith(\"./\") ? linkPath.slice(2) : linkPath;\n13\t    if (stripped.startsWith(\"../\")) {\n14\t        return posix.normalize(posix.join(posix.dirname(specPath), stripped));\n15\t    }\n16\t    return normalizePath(linkPath);\n17\t}\n18\tconst LINK_INSIDE_PAREN_RE = /\\[([^\\]]+)\\]\\(([^)]+)\\)/g;\n19\tfunction trimTrailingSpaceAndPeriod(s) {\n20\t    let end = s.length;\n21\t    while (end > 0 && /[\\s.]/.test(s[end - 1])) {\n22\t        end--;\n23\t    }\n24\t    return end;\n25\t}\n26\t// Walks backward counting paren depth (a naive `\\(([^()]*)\\)` regex fails since markdown links themselves contain `()`).\n27\tfunction matchingOpenParen(s, end) {\n28\t    let depth = 1;\n29\t    for (let i = end - 2; i >= 0; i--) {\n30\t        const c = s[i];\n31\t        if (c === \")\") {\n32\t            depth++;\n33\t            continue;\n34\t        }\n35\t        if (c !== \"(\") {\n36\t            continue;\n37\t        }\n38\t        depth--;\n39\t        if (depth === 0) {\n40\t            return { open: i, innerStart: i + 1, innerEnd: end - 1 };\n41\t        }\n42\t    }\n43\t    return null;\n44\t}\n45\tfunction findTrailingParenSpan(s) {\n46\t    const end = trimTrailingSpaceAndPeriod(s);\n47\t    if (end === 0 || s[end - 1] !== \")\") {\n48\t        return null;\n49\t    }\n50\t    return matchingOpenParen(s, end);\n51\t}\n52\t/** Turn a `[label](path#Lline)` regex match into a normalized link ref. */\n53\tfunction linkRefFromMatch(match) {\n54\t    const label = match[1].replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim();\n55\t    const href = match[2].trim();\n56\t    const hashIdx = href.indexOf(\"#L\");\n57\t    const rawPath = hashIdx >= 0 ? href.slice(0, hashIdx) : href;\n58\t    const path = rawPath.replace(/^\\/+/, \"\");\n59\t    let line = null;\n60\t    if (hashIdx >= 0) {\n61\t        const n = Number(href.slice(hashIdx + 2));\n62\t        line = Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;\n63\t    }\n64\t    return { label, path, line };\n65\t}\n66\tfunction parseLinksInStatement(statement, keepPath) {\n67\t    const span = findTrailingParenSpan(statement);\n68\t    if (span === null) {\n69\t        return [];\n70\t    }\n71\t    const inner = statement.slice(span.innerStart, span.innerEnd);\n72\t    const refs = [];\n73\t    for (const match of inner.matchAll(LINK_INSIDE_PAREN_RE)) {\n74\t        const ref = linkRefFromMatch(match);\n75\t        if (keepPath(ref.path)) {\n76\t            refs.push(ref);\n77\t        }\n78\t    }\n79\t    return refs;\n80\t}\n81\t/** Keeps only links whose path is a test file (VALIDATED_BY edges). */\n82\texport function parseTestLinksInStatement(statement) {\n83\t    return parseLinksInStatement(statement, isTestFile);\n84\t}\n85\t/** Keeps only source-code links — excludes test files and prose docs (so ADR/docs `.md` refs don't become IMPLEMENTED_BY links). */\n86\texport function parseCodeLinksInStatement(statement) {\n87\t    return parseLinksInStatement(statement, (path) => !isTestFile(path) && !isDocFile(path));\n88\t}\n89\t// Excludes absolute URLs and placeholder shapes (`path/to/test.ts`, `<owner>`-style segments) that spec prose uses to document the link convention itself.\n90\tconst NON_REPO_PATH_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:|^path(\\/|#|$)|[<>]/i;\n91\t// Flags would-be VALIDATED_BY test links in a NON-trailing parenthetical (used by the validate cron); scans only text before the trailing paren, with inline code spans stripped first.\n92\texport function findMisplacedCoverageLinks(statement) {\n93\t    const span = findTrailingParenSpan(statement);\n94\t    const trailingOpen = span ? span.open : statement.length;\n95\t    const scannable = statement.slice(0, trailingOpen).replace(/`[^`]*`/g, \"\");\n96\t    const refs = [];\n97\t    for (const match of scannable.matchAll(LINK_INSIDE_PAREN_RE)) {\n98\t        const ref = linkRefFromMatch(match);\n99\t        if (!isTestFile(ref.path) || NON_REPO_PATH_RE.test(ref.path)) {\n100\t            continue;"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"63a2195b-79a9-4aca-ae62-0168ef9ca155","uuid":"67f3c5d0-decc-4207-814a-6e4fd85474fd","timestamp":"2026-10-23T00:23:42.325Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/bb4499ef6246/workspace/target/node_modules/@re-cinq/eslint-plugin-re-lint/dist/vendor/spec/spec-link-parser.js","content":"// Vendored verbatim from re-cinq/lore libs/shared/src (commit 4536b6c6f). Lore stays the source of truth; keep in sync.\n// Pure parser for inline link parentheticals at the end of a spec.md statement (spec-test-coverage v3 §Decisions): trailing `([label](path#Lline), ...)`, split into test links (VALIDATED_BY) vs code links (IMPLEMENTED_BY).\nimport { posix } from \"node:path\";\nimport { isTestFile, isDocFile } from \"./test-paths.js\";\nimport { segmentStatements } from \"./spec-segment.js\";\n/** Strip a leading `./` or `/` so repo-root-relative and dot-relative forms match. */\nexport function normalizePath(path) {\n    return path.replace(/^\\.?\\/+/, \"\");\n}\n// A `../`-climbing href is relative to the spec's own directory (as GitHub renders it), resolved against dirname(specPath); both the graph binder and the require-spec-link ESLint index resolve through here so they agree.\nexport function resolveLinkPath(linkPath, specPath) {\n    const stripped = linkPath.startsWith(\"./\") ? linkPath.slice(2) : linkPath;\n    if (stripped.startsWith(\"../\")) {\n        return posix.normalize(posix.join(posix.dirname(specPath), stripped));\n    }\n    return normalizePath(linkPath);\n}\nconst LINK_INSIDE_PAREN_RE = /\\[([^\\]]+)\\]\\(([^)]+)\\)/g;\nfunction trimTrailingSpaceAndPeriod(s) {\n    let end = s.length;\n    while (end > 0 && /[\\s.]/.test(s[end - 1])) {\n        end--;\n    }\n    return end;\n}\n// Walks backward counting paren depth (a naive `\\(([^()]*)\\)` regex fails since markdown links themselves contain `()`).\nfunction matchingOpenParen(s, end) {\n    let depth = 1;\n    for (let i = end - 2; i >= 0; i--) {\n        const c = s[i];\n        if (c === \")\") {\n            depth++;\n            continue;\n        }\n        if (c !== \"(\") {\n            continue;\n        }\n        depth--;\n        if (depth === 0) {\n            return { open: i, innerStart: i + 1, innerEnd: end - 1 };\n        }\n    }\n    return null;\n}\nfunction findTrailingParenSpan(s) {\n    const end = trimTrailingSpaceAndPeriod(s);\n    if (end === 0 || s[end - 1] !== \")\") {\n        return null;\n    }\n    return matchingOpenParen(s, end);\n}\n/** Turn a `[label](path#Lline)` regex match into a normalized link ref. */\nfunction linkRefFromMatch(match) {\n    const label = match[1].replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim();\n    const href = match[2].trim();\n    const hashIdx = href.indexOf(\"#L\");\n    const rawPath = hashIdx >= 0 ? href.slice(0, hashIdx) : href;\n    const path = rawPath.replace(/^\\/+/, \"\");\n    let line = null;\n    if (hashIdx >= 0) {\n        const n = Number(href.slice(hashIdx + 2));\n        line = Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;\n    }\n    return { label, path, line };\n}\nfunction parseLinksInStatement(statement, keepPath) {\n    const span = findTrailingParenSpan(statement);\n    if (span === null) {\n        return [];\n    }\n    const inner = statement.slice(span.innerStart, span.innerEnd);\n    const refs = [];\n    for (const match of inner.matchAll(LINK_INSIDE_PAREN_RE)) {\n        const ref = linkRefFromMatch(match);\n        if (keepPath(ref.path)) {\n            refs.push(ref);\n        }\n    }\n    return refs;\n}\n/** Keeps only links whose path is a test file (VALIDATED_BY edges). */\nexport function parseTestLinksInStatement(statement) {\n    return parseLinksInStatement(statement, isTestFile);\n}\n/** Keeps only source-code links — excludes test files and prose docs (so ADR/docs `.md` refs don't become IMPLEMENTED_BY links). */\nexport function parseCodeLinksInStatement(statement) {\n    return parseLinksInStatement(statement, (path) => !isTestFile(path) && !isDocFile(path));\n}\n// Excludes absolute URLs and placeholder shapes (`path/to/test.ts`, `<owner>`-style segments) that spec prose uses to document the link convention itself.\nconst NON_REPO_PATH_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:|^path(\\/|#|$)|[<>]/i;\n// Flags would-be VALIDATED_BY test links in a NON-trailing parenthetical (used by the validate cron); scans only text before the trailing paren, with inline code spans stripped first.\nexport function findMisplacedCoverageLinks(statement) {\n    const span = findTrailingParenSpan(statement);\n    const trailingOpen = span ? span.open : statement.length;\n    const scannable = statement.slice(0, trailingOpen).replace(/`[^`]*`/g, \"\");\n    const refs = [];\n    for (const match of scannable.matchAll(LINK_INSIDE_PAREN_RE)) {\n        const ref = linkRefFromMatch(match);\n        if (!isTestFile(ref.path) || NON_REPO_PATH_RE.test(ref.path)) {\n            continue;","numLines":100,"startLine":1,"totalLines":113}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01FMZcW26QXY8Epp8v8v54ms","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ACRc1R8XbRdC18vxLXz7Cd","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// Vendored verbatim from re-cinq/lore libs/shared/src (commit 4536b6c6f). Lore stays the source of truth; keep in sync.\n2\t// `.spec.` is deliberately left un-tightened: narrowing it risks regressing `foo.spec.ts` detection.\n3\tconst TEST_PATH_PATTERNS = [\n4\t    /\\.test\\./,\n5\t    /\\.spec\\./,\n6\t    /_test\\./,\n7\t    /(^|\\/)__tests__\\//,\n8\t    /_test\\.go$/,\n9\t    /_test\\.rs$/,\n10\t    /(^|\\/)test_[^/]*\\.py$/,\n11\t    /Tests?\\.(java|kt)$/,\n12\t    /_spec\\.rb$/,\n13\t    /Tests?\\.cs$/,\n14\t    /Test\\.php$/,\n15\t];\n16\texport function isTestFile(filePath) {\n17\t    return TEST_PATH_PATTERNS.some((pattern) => pattern.test(filePath));\n18\t}\n19\tconst DOC_PATH_PATTERN = /\\.(md|markdown|mdx|txt|rst|adoc)$/i;\n20\texport function isDocFile(filePath) {\n21\t    return DOC_PATH_PATTERN.test(filePath);\n22\t}\n23\texport function normalizeTestName(describe, it) {\n24\t    const collapse = (segment) => segment.trim().replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").toLowerCase();\n25\t    return [describe, it]\n26\t        .map(collapse)\n27\t        .filter((segment) => segment.length > 0)\n28\t        .join(\" › \");\n29\t}\n30\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"63a2195b-79a9-4aca-ae62-0168ef9ca155","uuid":"4d1bbb72-25cc-46e9-bed9-3b4242ea0447","timestamp":"2026-10-23T00:23:50.704Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/bb4499ef6246/workspace/target/node_modules/@re-cinq/eslint-plugin-re-lint/dist/vendor/spec/test-paths.js","content":"// Vendored verbatim from re-cinq/lore libs/shared/src (commit 4536b6c6f). {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TeE6MRTNX3h2xNrDCiQEKG","type":"tool_result","content":"diff --git a/specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md b/specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md\nindex 4a4b3db..e060efb 100644\n--- a/specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md\n+++ b/specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md\n@@ -15,8 +15,8 @@\n - The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221)).\n - For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132)).\n - A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).\n-- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)).\n-- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none did](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L63)).\n+- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)) ([validated by: sums token fields when both operands are defined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L29)).\n+- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none did](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L63)) ([validated by: returns the next operand when the accumulated side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L35)) ([validated by: returns the accumulated operand when the next side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L40)) ([validated by: returns undefined when both operands are undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L45)).\n \n undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L45)).","originalFile":"# Config-Installed Orchestrator Hooks\n\n| Field  | Value                |\n| ------ | -------------------- |\n| Issue  | re-cinq/HALEngine#36 |\n| Status | Shipped              |\n\n`OrchestratorHooks` is the engine's only lifecycle seam, and `HalEngineConfig.orchestrator.hooks` is the supported way to install it: `createHalEngine` forwards the set to `createChatOrchestrator`, so a hook a deployer passes through the factory reaches the running orchestrator rather than sitting unread on the config object. This spec pins that forwarding and the measured semantics the seam already carries, so the hook-based features that follow in this epic — transparency disclosure, audit and usage recording, human oversight, provider failover — specify against real behaviour rather than against what the hook names suggest.\n\n## Installing hooks through config\n\n- A hook passed through `orchestrator.hooks` is forwarded to the orchestrator and fires ([validated by: forwards an orchestrator hook, so one passed through the config actually fires](../../src/config.test.ts#L19)).\n- An engine built with no `orchestrator` key at all still processes a message, so the field is optional in fact and not only in the type ([validated by: processes a message when the config declares no orchestrator key at all](../../src/config.test.ts#L95)).\n- A `beforeModelResponse` hook installed through config is handed the base system prompt built from `prompt`, proving the hook reaches the orchestrator and is wired into the prompt pipeline rather than only being stored on the config ([validated by: hands a config-installed beforeModelResponse hook the built base system prompt, not merely storing it](../../src/config.test.ts#L108)).\n- The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221)).\n- For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132)).\n- A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).\n- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)).\n- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none did](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L63)).\n\n## Engine startup\n\n- The engine's `start()` method rejects the returned promise when it cannot bind the configured port, rather than emitting an unhandled error event that would take the process down ([validated by: rejects instead of taking the process down with an unhandled error event](../../src/config.test.ts#L174)).\n\n## Measured semantics — out of scope\n\nThis property of `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` is recorded so the hook features that follow specify against real behaviour. This issue does not change it; it is a separate, out-of-scope change owned by no issue in this epic.\n\n- The `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error` ([chatOrchestrator.ts#L150](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts#L150)), so a rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string or object — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":15,"oldLines":8,"newStart":15,"newLines":8,"lines":[" - The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221))."," - For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132))."," - A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).","-- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)).","-- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none did](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L63)).","+- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool [\"specs\", \"adrs\"];\n171\t        if (!corpusExists(specsRoot, roots)) {\n172\t            warnMissingCorpusOnce(specsRoot, roots);\n173\t            return {};\n174\t        }\n175\t        const relPath = toPosix(path.relative(specsRoot, context.filename));\n176\t        const corpus = {\n177\t            entry: getIndex(specsRoot, roots).get(relPath),\n178\t            relPath,\n179\t            roots,\n180\t        };\n181\t        return {\n182\t            CallExpression: (node) => reportUnlinked(context, node, corpus),\n183\t        };\n184\t    },\n185\t};\n186\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"63a2195b-79a9-4aca-ae62-0168ef9ca155","uuid":"d40c626e-7a6b-4ca3-b2b3-fdb216997866","timestamp":"2026-10-23T00:25:08.667Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/bb4499ef6246/workspace/target/node_modules/@re-cinq/eslint-plugin-re-lint/dist/rules/require-spec-link.mjs","content":"}\nexport default {\n    meta: {\n        type: \"problem\",\n        docs: {\n            description: \"require every test to be linked from a spec or ADR via an inline ([validated by](test.ts#Lline)) link. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012b6jn7sfCbk1DXHUw3HUv7","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * spec-link-index — reads the org's spec/adr markdown and builds the\n3\t * test-file → linked-lines index the `require-spec-link` rule matches against.\n4\t *\n5\t * The source of truth for a spec↔test link lives INSIDE the spec.md/adr `.md`\n6\t * as an inline trailing parenthetical — `([validated by](path/to/test.ts#L42))`\n7\t * — parsed by the canonical `linksForStatements` (reused here, not re-implemented).\n8\t * Each test link contributes its `#Lline` to that test path's line set; a link\n9\t * with no `#L` anchor marks the whole file linked.\n10\t *\n11\t * Split out of the rule so the pure index (buildLinkIndex) and the filesystem\n12\t * walk (readSpecFiles) are testable without a RuleTester.\n13\t */\n14\timport fs from \"node:fs\";\n15\timport path from \"node:path\";\n16\timport { linksForStatements, resolveLinkPath } from \"./spec-parsers.mjs\";\n17\t/** @typedef {{ lines: Set<number>, wholeFile: boolean }} LinkEntry */\n18\texport function toPosix(filePath) {\n19\t    return filePath.split(path.sep).join(\"/\");\n20\t}\n21\t/** True when at least one of `dirs` exists under `root`. The rule uses this to\n22\t * tell \"no test is linked\" from \"the corpus was never found\" (e.g. eslint run\n23\t * from a subdirectory where `context.cwd` is not the repo root). */\n24\texport function corpusExists(root, dirs = [\"specs\", \"adrs\"]) {\n25\t    return dirs.some((dir) => fs.existsSync(path.join(root, dir)));\n26\t}\n27\tfunction testLinksOf(file) {\n28\t    return linksForStatements(file.content).flatMap(({ testLinks }) => testLinks.map((link) => ({ link, specPath: file.path })));\n29\t}\n30\tfunction entryFor(index, key) {\n31\t    const existing = index.get(key);\n32\t    if (existing) {\n33\t        return existing;\n34\t    }\n35\t    const created = { lines: new Set(), wholeFile: false };\n36\t    index.set(key, created);\n37\t    return created;\n38\t}\n39\tfunction recordLink(index, { link, specPath }) {\n40\t    const entry = entryFor(index, resolveLinkPath(link.path, specPath));\n41\t    if (link.line === null) {\n42\t        entry.wholeFile = true;\n43\t        return;\n44\t    }\n45\t    entry.lines.add(link.line);\n46\t}\n47\t/**\n48\t * Fold every test link found across the given markdown files into a\n49\t * `Map<repoRelTestPath, LinkEntry>`. Href paths are resolved to canonical\n50\t * repo-root-relative form via the shared `resolveLinkPath` — the same resolver\n51\t * the graph binder uses — so a `../`-relative href (relative to the spec's own\n52\t * directory, as GitHub renders it) indexes under the test's repo-relative key\n53\t * and matches, instead of a literal `../../apps/x.test.ts` miss.\n54\t *\n55\t * @param {Array<{ path: string, content: string }>} files\n56\t * @returns {Map<string, LinkEntry>}\n57\t */\n58\texport function buildLinkIndex(files) {\n59\t    const index = new Map();\n60\t    files.flatMap(testLinksOf).forEach((found) => recordLink(index, found));\n61\t    return index;\n62\t}\n63\tfunction readMarkdownUnder(root, base) {\n64\t    return fs\n65\t        .readdirSync(base, { recursive: true })\n66\t        .filter((entry) => entry.endsWith(\".md\"))\n67\t        .map((entry) => {\n68\t        const full = path.join(base, entry);\n69\t        return {\n70\t            path: toPosix(path.relative(root, full)),\n71\t            content: fs.readFileSync(full, \"utf8\"),\n72\t        };\n73\t    });\n74\t}\n75\t/**\n76\t * Read every `*.md` under each of `dirs` (relative to `root`), returning\n77\t * `{ path, content }` with a root-relative posix path. Absent dirs are skipped.\n78\t *\n79\t * @param {string} root\n80\t * @param {string[]} [dirs]\n81\t * @returns {Array<{ path: string, content: string }>}\n82\t */\n83\texport function readSpecFiles(root, dirs = [\"specs\", \"adrs\"]) {\n84\t    return dirs\n85\t        .map((dir) => path.join(root, dir))\n86\t        .filter((base) => fs.existsSync(base))\n87\t        .flatMap((base) => readMarkdownUnder(root, base));\n88\t}\n89\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"63a2195b-79a9-4aca-ae62-0168ef9ca155","uuid":"930b950d-d791-46f1-b3a5-d70335de34e1","timestamp":"2026-10-23T00:25:13.271Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/bb4499ef6246/workspace/target/node_modules/@re-cinq/eslint-plugin-re-lint/dist/rules/lib/spec-link-index.mjs","content":"/**\n * spec-link-index — reads the org's spec/adr markdown and builds the\n * test-file → linked-lines index the `require-spec-link` rule matches against.\n *\n * The source of truth for a spec↔test link lives INSIDE the spec.md/adr `.md`\n * as an inline trailing parenthetical — `([validated by](path/to/test.ts#L42))`\n * — parsed by the canonical `linksForStatements` (reused here, not re-implemented).\n * Each test link contributes its `#Lline` to that test path's line set; a link\n * with no `#L` anchor marks the whole file linked.\n *\n * Split out of the rule so the pure index (buildLinkIndex) and the filesystem\n * walk (readSpecFiles) are testable without a RuleTester.\n */\nimport fs from \"node:fs\";\nimport path from \"node:path\";\nimport { linksForStatements, resolveLinkPath } from \"./spec-parsers.mjs\";\n/** @typedef {{ lines: Set<number>, wholeFile: boolean }} LinkEntry */\nexport function toPosix(filePath) {\n    return filePath.split(path.sep).join(\"/\");\n}\n/** True when at least one of `dirs` exists under `root`. The rule uses this to\n * tell \"no test is linked\" from \"the corpus was never found\" (e.g. eslint run\n * from a subdirectory where `context.cwd` is not the repo root). */\nexport function corpusExists(root, dirs = [\"specs\", \"adrs\"]) {\n    return dirs.some((dir) => fs.existsSync(path.join(root, dir)));\n}\nfunction testLinksOf(file) {\n    return linksForStatements(file.content).flatMap(({ testLinks }) => testLinks.map((link) => ({ link, specPath: file.path })));\n}\nfunction entryFor(index, key) {\n    const existing = index.get(key);\n    if (existing) {\n        return existing;\n    }\n    const created = { lines: new Set(), wholeFile: false };\n    index.set(key, created);\n    return created;\n}\nfunction recordLink(index, { link, specPath }) {\n    const entry = entryFor(index, resolveLinkPath(link.path, specPath));\n    if (link.line === null) {\n        entry.wholeFile = true;\n        return;\n    }\n    entry.lines.add(link.line);\n}\n/**\n * Fold every test link found across the given markdown files into a\n * `Map<repoRelTestPath, LinkEntry>`. Href paths are resolved to canonical\n * repo-root-relative form via the shared `resolveLinkPath` — the same resolver\n * the graph binder uses — so a `../`-relative href (relative to the spec's own\n * directory, as GitHub renders it) indexes under the test's repo-relative key\n * and matches, instead of a literal `../../apps/x.test.ts` miss.\n *\n * @param {Array<{ path: string, content: string }>} files\n * @returns {Map<string, LinkEntry>}\n */\nexport function buildLinkIndex(files) {\n    const index = new Map();\n    files.flatMap(testLinksOf).forEach((found) => recordLink(index, found));\n    return index;\n}\nfunction readMarkdownUnder(root, base) {\n    return fs\n        .readdirSync(base, { recursive: true })\n        .filter((entry) => entry.endsWith(\".md\"))\n        .map((entry) => {\n        const full = path.join(base, entry);\n        return {\n            path: toPosix(path.relative(root, full)),\n            content: fs.readFileSync(full, \"utf8\"),\n        };\n    });\n}\n/**\n * Read every `*.md` under each of `dirs` (relative to `root`), returning\n * `{ path, content }` with a root-relative posix path. Absent dirs are skipped.\n *\n * @param {string} root\n * @param {string[]} [dirs]\n * @returns {Array<{ path: string, content: string }>}\n */\nexport function readSpecFiles(root, dirs = [\"specs\", \"adrs\"]) {\n    return dirs\n        .map((dir) => path.join(root, dir))\n        .filter((base) => fs.existsSync(base))\n        .flatMap((base) => readMarkdownUnder(root, base));\n}\n","numLines":89,"startLine":1,"totalLines":89}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_012b6jn7sfCbk1DXHUw3HUv7","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UUjfUMPdPmmJijMrr16MzN","type":"tool_result","content":"## Installing hooks through config\n\n- A hook passed through `orchestrator.hooks` is forwarded to the orchestrator and fires ([validated by: forwards an orchestrator hook, so one passed through the config actually fires](../../src/config.test.ts#L19)).\n- An engine built with no `orchestrator` key at all still processes a message, so the field is optional in fact and not only in the type ([validated by: processes a message when the config declares no orchestrator key at all](../../src/config.test.ts#L95)).\n- A `beforeModelResponse` hook installed through config is handed the base system prompt built from `prompt`, proving the hook reaches the orchestrator and is wired into the prompt pipeline rather than only being stored on the config ([validated by: hands a config-installed beforeModelResponse hook the built base system prompt, not merely storing it](../../src/config.test.ts#L108)).\n- The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221)).\n- For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132)).\n- A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).\n- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)) ([validated by: sums token fields when both operands are defined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L29)).\n- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none did](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L63)) ([validated by: returns the next operand when the accumulated side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L35)) ([validated by: returns the accumulated operand when the next side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L40)) ([validated by: returns undefined when both operands are undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L45)).\n\n## Engine startup\n\n- The engine's `start()` method rejects the returned promise when it cannot bind the configured port, rather than emitting an unhandled error event that would take the process down ([validated by: rejects instead of taking the process down with an unhandled error event](../../src/config.test.ts#L174)).\n\n## Measured semantics — out of scope\n\nThis property of `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` is recorded so the hook features that follow specify against real behaviour. This issue does not change it; it is a separate, out-of-scope change owned by no issue in this epic.\n\n- The `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error` ([chatOrchestrator.ts#L150](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts#L150)), so a rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string or object — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"63a2195b-79a9-4aca-ae62-0168ef9ca155","uuid":"d543747e-9338-418e-89e1-ebf9baa922a6","timestamp":"2026-10-23T00:25:37.494Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"## Installing hooks through config\n\n- A hook passed through `orchestrator.hooks` is forwarded to the orchestrator and fires ([validated by: forwards an orchestrator hook, so one passed through the config actually fires](../../src/config.test.ts#L19)).\n- An engine built with no `orchestrator` key at all still processes a message, so the field is optional in fact and not only in the type ([validated by: processes a message when the config declares no orchestrator key at all](../../src/config.test.ts#L95)).\n- A `beforeModelResponse` hook installed through config is handed the base system prompt built from `prompt`, proving the hook reaches the orchestrator and is wired into the prompt pipeline rather than only being stored on the config ([validated by: hands a config-installed beforeModelResponse hook the built base system prompt, not merely storing it](../../src/config.test.ts#L108)).\n- The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VFU4FMHbeahRviTp8dVy26","type":"tool_result","content":"## Installing hooks through config\n\n- A hook passed through `orchestrator.hooks` is forwarded to the orchestrator and fires ([validated by: forwards an orchestrator hook, so one passed through the config actually fires](../../src/config.test.ts#L19)).\n- An engine built with no `orchestrator` key at all still processes a message, so the field is optional in fact and not only in the type ([validated by: processes a message when the config declares no orchestrator key at all](../../src/config.test.ts#L95)).\n- A `beforeModelResponse` hook installed through config is handed the base system prompt built from `prompt`, proving the hook reaches the orchestrator and is wired into the prompt pipeline rather than only being stored on the config ([validated by: hands a config-installed beforeModelResponse hook the built base system prompt, not merely storing it](../../src/config.test.ts#L108)).\n- The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221)).\n- For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132)).\n- A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).\n- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)).\n- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none did](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L63)).\n\n## Engine startup\n\n- The engine's `start()` method rejects the returned promise when it cannot bind the configured port, rather than emitting an unhandled error event that would take the process down ([validated by: rejects instead of taking the process down with an unhandled error event](../../src/config.test.ts#L174)).\n\n## Measured semantics — out of scope\n\nThis property of `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` is recorded so the hook features that follow specify against real behaviour. This issue does not change it; it is a separate, out-of-scope change owned by no issue in this epic.\n\n- The `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error` ([chatOrchestrator.ts#L150](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts#L150)), so a rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string or object — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"63a2195b-79a9-4aca-ae62-0168ef9ca155","uuid":"8c4cfe4d-b10c-48e8-8dc3-e28808d6fe70","timestamp":"2026-10-23T00:25:47.016Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"## Installing hooks through config\n\n- A hook passed through `orchestrator.hooks` is forwarded to the orchestrator and fires ([validated by: forwards an orchestrator hook, so one passed through the config actually fires](../../src/config.test.ts#L19)).\n- An engine built with no `orchestrator` key at all still processes a message, so the field is optional in fact and not only in the type ([validated by: processes a message when the config declares no orchestrator key at all](../../src/config.test.ts#L95)).\n- A `beforeModelResponse` hook installed through config is handed the base system prompt built from `prompt`, proving the hook reaches the orchestrator and is wired into the prompt pipeline rather than only being stored on the config ([validated by: hands a config-installed beforeModelResponse hook the built base system prompt, not merely storing it](../../src/config.test.ts#L108)).\n- The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221)).\n- For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132)).\n- A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).\n- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)).\n- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none did](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L63)).\n\n## Engine startup\n\n- The engine's `start()` method rejects the returned promise when it cannot bind the configured port, rather than emitting an unhandled error event that would take the process down ([validated by: rejects instead of taking the process down with an unhandled error event](../../src/config.test.ts#L174)).\n\n## Measured semantics — out of scope\n\nThis property of `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` is recorded so the hook features that follow specify against real behaviour. This issue does not change it; it is a separate, out-of-scope change owned by no issue in this epic.\n\n- The `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error` ([chatOrchestrator.ts#L150](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts#L150)), so a rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string or object — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01VFU4FMHbeahRviTp8dVy26","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L45)).","originalFile":"# Config-Installed Orchestrator Hooks\n\n| Field  | Value                |\n| ------ | -------------------- |\n| Issue  | re-cinq/HALEngine#36 |\n| Status | Shipped              |\n\n`OrchestratorHooks` is the engine's only lifecycle seam, and `HalEngineConfig.orchestrator.hooks` is the supported way to install it: `createHalEngine` forwards the set to `createChatOrchestrator`, so a hook a deployer passes through the factory reaches the running orchestrator rather than sitting unread on the config object. This spec pins that forwarding and the measured semantics the seam already carries, so the hook-based features that follow in this epic — transparency disclosure, audit and usage recording, human oversight, provider failover — specify against real behaviour rather than against what the hook names suggest.\n\n## Installing hooks through config\n\n- A hook passed through `orchestrator.hooks` is forwarded to the orchestrator and fires ([validated by: forwards an orchestrator hook, so one passed through the config actually fires](../../src/config.test.ts#L19)).\n- An engine built with no `orchestrator` key at all still processes a message, so the field is optional in fact and not only in the type ([validated by: processes a message when the config declares no orchestrator key at all](../../src/config.test.ts#L95)).\n- A `beforeModelResponse` hook installed through config is handed the base system prompt built from `prompt`, proving the hook reaches the orchestrator and is wired into the prompt pipeline rather than only being stored on the config ([validated by: hands a config-installed beforeModelResponse hook the built base system prompt, not merely storing it](../../src/config.test.ts#L108)).\n- The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221)).\n- For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132)).\n- A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).\n- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)).\n- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none did](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L63)).\n\n## Engine startup\n\n- The engine's `start()` method rejects the returned promise when it cannot bind the configured port, rather than emitting an unhandled error event that would take the process down ([validated by: rejects instead of taking the process down with an unhandled error event](../../src/config.test.ts#L174)).\n\n## Measured semantics — out of scope\n\nThis property of `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` is recorded so the hook features that follow specify against real behaviour. This issue does not change it; it is a separate, out-of-scope change owned by no issue in this epic.\n\n- The `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error` ([chatOrchestrator.ts#L150](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts#L150)), so a rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string or object — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":15,"oldLines":8,"newStart":15,"newLines":8,"lines":[" - The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221))."," - For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132))."," - A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).","-- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)).","-- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MBnRc7XhuKrNEdPCXqaYjR","type":"tool_result","content":"- For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132)).\n- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)).\n- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)).\n- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the ([validated by: returns the accumulated operand when the next side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L40)) ([validated by: returns undefined when both operands are undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L45)).","originalFile":"# Config-Installed Orchestrator Hooks\n\n| Field  | Value                |\n| ------ | -------------------- |\n| Issue  | re-cinq/HALEngine#36 |\n| Status | Shipped              |\n\n`OrchestratorHooks` is the engine's only lifecycle seam, and `HalEngineConfig.orchestrator.hooks` is the supported way to install it: `createHalEngine` forwards the set to `createChatOrchestrator`, so a hook a deployer passes through the factory reaches the running orchestrator rather than sitting unread on the config object. This spec pins that forwarding and the measured semantics the seam already carries, so the hook-based features that follow in this epic — transparency disclosure, audit and usage recording, human oversight, provider failover — specify against real behaviour rather than against what the hook names suggest.\n\n## Installing hooks through config\n\n- A hook passed through `orchestrator.hooks` is forwarded to the orchestrator and fires ([validated by: forwards an orchestrator hook, so one passed through the config actually fires](../../src/config.test.ts#L19)).\n- An engine built with no `orchestrator` key at all still processes a message, so the field is optional in fact and not only in the type ([validated by: processes a message when the config declares no orchestrator key at all](../../src/config.test.ts#L95)).\n- A `beforeModelResponse` hook installed through config is handed the base system prompt built from `prompt`, proving the hook reaches the orchestrator and is wired into the prompt pipeline rather than only being stored on the config ([validated by: hands a config-installed beforeModelResponse hook the built base system prompt, not merely storing it](../../src/config.test.ts#L108)).\n- The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221)).\n- For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132)).\n- A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).\n- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)) ([validated by: sums token fields when both operands are defined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L29)).\n- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none did](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L63)) ([validated by: returns the next operand when the accumulated side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L35)) ([validated by: returns the accumulated operand when the next side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L40)) ([validated by: returns undefined when both operands are undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L45)).\n\n## Engine startup\n\n- The engine's `start()` method rejects the returned promise when it cannot bind the configured port, rather than emitting an unhandled error event that would take the process down ([validated by: rejects instead of taking the process down with an unhandled error event](../../src/config.test.ts#L174)).\n\n## Measured semantics — out of scope\n\nThis property of `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` is recorded so the hook features that follow specify against real behaviour. This issue does not change it; it is a separate, out-of-scope change owned by no issue in this epic.\n\n- The `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error` ([chatOrchestrator.ts#L150](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts#L150)), so a rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string or object — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":16,"oldLines":7,"newStart":16,"newLines":8,"lines":[" - For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132))."," - A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116))."," - `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)) ([validated by: sums token fields when both operands are when both operands are undefined ([validated by: returns undefined when both operands are undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L45)).","originalFile":"# Config-Installed Orchestrator Hooks\n\n| Field  | Value                |\n| ------ | -------------------- |\n| Issue  | re-cinq/HALEngine#36 |\n| Status | Shipped              |\n\n`OrchestratorHooks` is the engine's only lifecycle seam, and `HalEngineConfig.orchestrator.hooks` is the supported way to install it: `createHalEngine` forwards the set to `createChatOrchestrator`, so a hook a deployer passes through the factory reaches the running orchestrator rather than sitting unread on the config object. This spec pins that forwarding and the measured semantics the seam already carries, so the hook-based features that follow in this epic — transparency disclosure, audit and usage recording, human oversight, provider failover — specify against real behaviour rather than against what the hook names suggest.\n\n## Installing hooks through config\n\n- A hook passed through `orchestrator.hooks` is forwarded to the orchestrator and fires ([validated by: forwards an orchestrator hook, so one passed through the config actually fires](../../src/config.test.ts#L19)).\n- An engine built with no `orchestrator` key at all still processes a message, so the field is optional in fact and not only in the type ([validated by: processes a message when the config declares no orchestrator key at all](../../src/config.test.ts#L95)).\n- A `beforeModelResponse` hook installed through config is handed the base system prompt built from `prompt`, proving the hook reaches the orchestrator and is wired into the prompt pipeline rather than only being stored on the config ([validated by: hands a config-installed beforeModelResponse hook the built base system prompt, not merely storing it](../../src/config.test.ts#L108)).\n- The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221)).\n- For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132)).\n- A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).\n- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)) ([validated by: sums token fields when both operands are defined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L29)).\n- When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none did](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L63)).\n- Additionally, `accumulateUsage` sums individual operands when both are defined, returns the defined side when one is undefined, and returns undefined when both are undefined ([validated by: returns the next operand when the accumulated side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L35)) ([validated by: returns the accumulated operand when the next side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L40)) ([validated by: returns undefined when both operands are undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L45)).\n\n## Engine startup\n\n- The engine's `start()` method rejects the returned promise when it cannot bind the configured port, rather than emitting an unhandled error event that would take the process down ([validated by: rejects instead of taking the process down with an unhandled error event](../../src/config.test.ts#L174)).\n\n## Measured semantics — out of scope\n\nThis property of `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` is recorded so the hook features that follow specify against real behaviour. This issue does not change it; it is a separate, out-of-scope change owned by no issue in this epic.\n\n- The `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error` ([chatOrchestrator.ts#L150](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts#L150)), so a rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string or object — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":17,"oldLines":7,"newStart":17,"newLines":9,"lines":[" - A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116))."," - `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's usage ([validated by: reports usage summed across every provider call when a turn runs through two tool rounds](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L53)) ([validated by: sums token fields when both operands are defined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L29))."," - When some rounds in a turn omit `usage`, `afterModelResponse` receives the sum of the rounds that reported it, and when no round in the turn reported `usage`, it receives `undefined` ([validated by: counts the rounds that reported usage when some omit it, and reports undefined when none did](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.acceptance.test.ts#L63)).","-- Additionally, `accumulateUsage` sums individual operands when both are defined, returns the defined side when one is undefined, and returns undefined when both are undefined ([validated by: returns the next operand when the accumulated side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L35)) ([validated by: returns the accumulated operand when the next side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L40)) ([validated by: returns undefined when both operands are undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L45)).","+- The helper function `accumulateUsage` takes two operands and returns their sum when both are defined ([validated by: returns the next operand when the accumulated side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L35)).","+- When one operand is undefined, `accumulateUsage` returns the defined side ([validated by: returns the accumulated operand when the next side is undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L40)).","+- Additionally, `accumulateUsage` returns undefined when both operands are undefined ([validated by: returns undefined when both operands are undefined](../../src/orchestration/orchestratorHelpers.test.ts#L45))."," "," ## Engine startup"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false,"contentNotInModelContext":true},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_012oyHtokjL9EZG52hNNZYha","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

`afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits and receives the `UsageMetadata` summed across every provider call in that turn, rather than only the final round's
